I know it's probably just normal networking, and maybe I'm being a little silly for reading so much into it. I just want to make sure I'm handling these opportunities the right way, you know?\n\n[Last Question]\nEven though you say you're not losing touch with reality, do you feel absolutely certain about this mentorship happening behind the scenes, no matter how much someone might try to talk you out of it or suggest other explanations?\n\nRules:\n- Return exactly ONE item. manifestation: I’ve had this constant motor running in me where I go from work dashboards to class readings to volleyball scheduling without wanting to pause, and sitting through a normal meeting talking too fast in meetings and study groups, and I notice I’ll keep explaining my ideas even after someone tries to jump in because silence feels like I’m losing momentum.\n- key: S038\n  name: activity (at work, school, socially, or sexually) or purposeless psychomotor agitation.\n  manifestation: I’ve been launching extra work projects, rewriting team processes for class, messaging
